--- Bug Hunter re-file (2026-07-08 11:07) - file was overwritten by previous agent loop ---

- [ ] **[PLG-010]** `[Build-Breaking / Missing Import]` ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs:294 uses Task.Run without using System.Threading.Tasks. *Root Cause*: ShellIntegrationPlugin.cs has the import but ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs does not, causing a build error when Task.Run is invoked. *Suggested Fix*: Add `using System.Threading.Tasks;` to the top of ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs.

- [ ] **[PLG-011]** `[Test Infrastructure / Build-Breaking]` BatteryHealthServiceTests.cs:125,152 accesses task.Result after await Task.WhenAll, triggering xUnit1031 build error. *Root Cause*: After await Task.WhenAll, each individual task is already completed, so accessing .Result is redundant and triggers the xUnit analyzer warning/error. *Suggested Fix*: Replace `task.Result` with the directly awaited value or use `task.GetAwaiter().GetResult()` since the task is known to be completed.

- [ ] **[PLG-012]** `[Async Void / Crash Risk]` PluginWorkbench MainWindow.xaml.cs has 7 unguarded async void handlers at L76,115,123,153,175,180,188. *Root Cause*: Of 9 async void WPF event handlers, only BootstrapHostButton_Click (L139) and RunOptimizationActionButton_Click (L245) have try-catch/try-finally wrappers. The other 7 handlers perform async I/O (plugin loading, file dialogs) without exception handling. An unhandled exception in async void crashes the process. *Suggested Fix*: Wrap each handler body in try-catch with logging: `try { ... } catch (Exception ex) { System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(ex); }`

- [ ] **[PLG-013]** `[Async Void / Crash Risk]` 2 additional async void handlers without try-catch in plugins: BatteryHealthControl.xaml.cs:L155 (RefreshButton_Click) and ViveToolPage.xaml.cs:L1122 (StatusFilterComboBox_SelectionChanged). *Root Cause*: Like PLG-012, these handlers perform async operations without try-catch wrappers. StatusFilterComboBox fires on every keystroke, making it especially crash-prone. *Suggested Fix*: Wrap each handler body in try-catch with logging.

- [x] **[PLG-014]** `[i18n / Hardcoded Strings]` Plugin XAML files contain hardcoded English strings not extracted to Resource.resx across ViveTool, ShellIntegration, NetworkAcceleration, and CustomMouse plugins. *Root Cause*: Plugin pages have hardcoded Content="Refresh", Content="Save", Content="Download", etc. directly in XAML instead of using x:Uid with Resource.resx entries. The main UDT app uses Resource.resx for all UI strings, but plugins do not follow the same pattern. *Suggested Fix*: Add Resource.resx entries for all user-facing strings and use x:Uid markup extensions in XAML.

_Resolution block (recovery + PLG-015/016, 2026-07-08, UI/UX Visual-Polish audit per maintainer authorization): A prior in-place edit of this ledger corrupted its line structure (newlines stripped, file collapsed to 1 line); the ledger was restored from `git restore .bugs/1_NEW_REPORTS.md` to HEAD (4410 lines), then this authoritative block was re-appended with `Add-Content`. The source-code fix below survived intact (only the ledger text was corrupted)._

- [x] **[PLG-015]** `[Governance / Hardcoded Hex Colors — RESOLVED, wired]` `Tools\PluginCompletionUiTool\MainWindow.xaml` previously had 7 hardcoded hex color literals (L24/L27/L138/L196/L198/L204/L205 of `#FFD0D0D0`/`#FFF8F8F8`/`#FF0D1117`/`#FFE6EDF3`) plus a `Background="White"` sibling (L141). *Root Cause*: the standalone tool had no WPF-UI theme dictionaries merged, so colors were hard-coded. *Resolution (maintainer-authorized option b: wire Fluent theming)*: (1) `PluginCompletionUiTool.csproj` now references `<PackageReference Include="WPF-UI" Version="4.3.0" />` (mirrors sibling `PluginWorkbench.csproj`); (2) `App.xaml` rewritten to merge `<wpfui:ControlsDictionary />` + `<wpfui:ThemesDictionary Theme="Dark" />` plus `xmlns:wpfui` (mirrors canonical `PluginWorkbench\App.xaml`); (3) `MainWindow.xaml` 7 hex literals + `White` replaced with `{DynamicResource ControlStrokeColorDefaultBrush}` / `{DynamicResource ControlFillColorDefaultBrush}` / `{DynamicResource ControlFillColorSecondaryBrush}` / `{DynamicResource TextFillColorPrimaryBrush}` (now 8 DynamicResource refs, 0 hex literals). *Verification*: `dotnet build PluginCompletionUiTool.csproj` => 0 warnings / 0 errors (WPF-UI 4.3.0 restored; tokens resolve via merged `ThemesDictionary`). Ticket CLOSED.

- [x] **[PLG-016]** `[UI/UX / Retracted-False-Positive]` ~~NetworkAccelerationControl.xaml monolith~~ — CLOSED on re-verification. NetworksAccelerationControl.xaml is 724 lines with a `TabControl` (L144) holding 2 `TabItem`s (Dashboard L184, Optimization L411), each in its own `<ScrollViewer>`. All cards use `CornerRadius` + `{DynamicResource}` binding. The L492/L541 `MaxWidth="340"` sites are `TextWrapping="Wrap"` description boxes (wrap, NOT ellipsis). The pass-1 "monolith + clip" claim was a line-count/keyword false positive that missed the tab boundaries. No defect remains. The ID is CONSUMED; the next unstamped ticket should be **PLG-017** to avoid a duplicate-ID collision.

- [x] **[PLG-014]** `[i18n / Hardcoded Strings — RESOLVED via locale backfill]` Plugin XAML files previously had hardcoded English strings not extracted to Resource.resx. *Root Cause (revised)*: Full re-scan of all plugin runtime XAML found 0 hardcoded Content/Text literals -- the prior premise described a legacy state already fixed. Every plugin binds text via `x:Static text:*Text.*` (a typed resource wrapper over Resource.resx). The genuine residual gap was *priority-locale coverage*: BatteryHealth lacked `de`/`ja`/`ru` satellite files (only had `en` + `zh-Hans`); the other 4 plugins already had all 4 priority locales. *Resolution (Phase 1b, 2026-07-08, maintainer-authorized)*: Created `Plugins\BatteryHealth\Resources\Resource.de.resx`, `Resource.ja.resx`, `Resource.ru.resx` (45 keys each, full translations), mirroring the simple-resheader format of the existing `Resource.en.resx`/`Resource.zh-Hans.resx`. SDK auto-embeds them as satellite assemblies (no csproj change needed -- `zh-Hans.resx` already worked that way). Coverage now: ALL 5 plugins (BatteryHealth/CustomMouse/NetworkAcceleration/ShellIntegration/ViveTool) carry complete `zh-Hans`/`de`/`ja`/`ru` coverage. *Verification*: `dotnet build` (BatteryHealth) => 0 warnings / 0 errors; `obj\Debug` satellite DLLs emitted for `de`/`en`/`ja`/`ru`/`zh-Hans` (`*.resources.dll`). Ticket CLOSED.

- [ ] **[PLG-017]** [Thread Safety / UI Freeze] Sync-over-async blocks the WPF Dispatcher in Plugins\ShellIntegration\ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs:295 (handler SyncManagedConfigButton_Click at L293-297). *Root Cause*: The handler is a synchronous private void ...Click that calls Task.Run(async () => await _plugin.SyncManagedConfigurationAsync()).GetAwaiter().GetResult() directly on the UI/Dispatcher thread. Although Task.Run offloads the async work to the ThreadPool (avoiding the classic SynchronizationContext self-deadlock of a bare .Result/.Wait()), .GetAwaiter().GetResult() still **synchronously blocks the Dispatcher pump** until the config sync completes; any synchronous filesystem/registry write inside SyncManagedConfigurationAsync() (ShellIntegrationPlugin.cs:666) can therefore freeze the host WPF window (clock cursor / non-responsive) for the full I/O duration. This violates Pillar B Governance A (async UI handlers must not block the Dispatcher) and is inconsistent with its two sibling handlers in the same file — EnableButton_Click (L233) and DisableButton_Click (L247) — which are both correctly private async void with try-catch. *Suggested Fix*: Convert the handler to sync void with a try-catch guard, mirroring the siblings:
`csharp
private async void SyncManagedConfigButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
{
    try
    {
        var success = await _plugin.SyncManagedConfigurationAsync().ConfigureAwait(true);
        RefreshStatus(success ? ShellIntegrationText.StatusManagedConfigSyncCompleted : ShellIntegrationText.StatusManagedConfigSyncFailed, !success);
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
        RefreshStatus($"{ShellIntegrationText.ErrorPrefix}: {ex.Message}", true);
        PluginLog.Trace($"SyncManagedConfigButton_Click error: {ex.Message}", ex);
    }
}
`
*Verification*: dotnet build (Release) = 0 warnings/0 errors; dotnet test ShellIntegration.Tests green; manual: click "Sync Managed Config" repeatedly — window remains responsive (no clock cursor) during sync.
